### [1] [Can LLMs Reason Like Automated Theorem Provers for Rust Verification? VCoT-Bench: Evaluating via Verification Chain of Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18334)
*Zichen Xie,Wenxi Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出VCoT-Lift框架，将低级求解器推理提升为人类可读的高层验证步骤，并构建VCoT-Bench基准（1,988个任务）用于细粒度评估LLMs在Rust程序验证中的推理能力。研究发现当前顶尖LLMs在此任务上表现脆弱，远不及自动定理证明器的推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估将Rust验证视为黑盒，仅通过证明提示的二元通过/失败结果来评估模型，这无法揭示模型是否真正理解验证所需的逻辑推理。亟需一种细粒度评估方法来判断LLMs是否具备验证非平凡Rust代码的推理能力。

Method: 提出VCoT-Lift框架，将底层求解器推理显式化为验证思维链（Verification Chain-of-Thought），提供高层人类可读的验证步骤作为细粒度评估的真实依据。基于此框架构建VCoT-Bench基准，包含1,988个VCoT补全任务，从三个正交维度评估：1)对不同程度缺失证明的鲁棒性；2)不同证明类型的能力；3)对证明位置的敏感性。

Result: 对10个最先进的LLM进行评估，结果显示所有模型均表现出严重的脆弱性，在验证推理任务上表现远不如自动定理证明器。

Conclusion: 当前LLMs在Rust程序验证任务上缺乏真正的逻辑推理能力，仅能进行表面的证明提示生成，无法达到自动定理证明器的推理水平。该研究为评估LLMs在形式化验证领域的应用提供了重要的细粒度评估框架和基准。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly assist secure software development, their ability to meet the rigorous demands of Rust program verification remains unclear. Existing evaluations treat Rust verification as a black box, assessing models only by binary pass or fail outcomes for proof hints. This obscures whether models truly understand the logical deductions required for verifying nontrivial Rust code. To bridge this gap, we introduce VCoT-Lift, a framework that lifts low-level solver reasoning into high-level, human-readable verification steps. By exposing solver-level reasoning as an explicit Verification Chain-of-Thought, VCoT-Lift provides a concrete ground truth for fine-grained evaluation. Leveraging VCoT-Lift, we introduce VCoT-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark of 1,988 VCoT completion tasks for rigorously evaluating LLMs' understanding of the entire verification process. VCoT-Bench measures performance along three orthogonal dimensions: robustness to varying degrees of missing proofs, competence across different proof types, and sensitivity to the proof locations. Evaluation of ten state-of-the-art models reveals severe fragility, indicating that current LLMs fall well short of the reasoning capabilities exhibited by automated theorem provers.

</details>


### [2] [Where are the Hidden Gems? Applying Transformer Models for Design Discussion Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18393)
*Lawrence Arkoh,Daniel Feitosa,Wesley K. G. Assunção*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究探索了Transformer模型在跨领域软件设计讨论检测中的表现，在Stack Overflow上训练并在GitHub artifacts上评估。BERT和RoBERTa表现较强 recall，ChatGPT-4o-mini获得最高recall和数据增强未带来显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程中的设计决策出现在Q&A论坛、邮件列表、PR、issue和提交信息中，对重构和现代化至关重要。现有机器学习方法受限于标注数据稀缺和高标注成本，Transformer模型在此跨领域设置中尚未被充分探索。

Method: 对既往跨域研究进行概念复制，并扩展至现代Transformer架构(BERT、RoBERTa、XLNet、LaMini-Flan-T5-77M、ChatGPT-4o-mini)。在Stack Overflow上微调，在GitHub artifacts(PR、issues、commits)上评估。同时测试了相似词注入数据增强方法。

Result: BERT和RoBERTa跨域recall表现强；XLNet precision高但recall低；ChatGPT-4o-mini获得最高recall和具有竞争力的整体表现；LaMini-Flan-T5-77M作为轻量替代方案，precision较强但性能不够均衡；相似词注入数据增强未产生有意义提升。

Conclusion: 结果凸显了现代语言模型在设计讨论检测中的机遇与权衡，为跨领域设计决策自动识别提供了实践参考。

Abstract: Design decisions are at the core of software engineering and appear in Q\&A forums, mailing lists, pull requests, issue trackers, and commit messages. Design discussions spanning a project's history provide valuable information for informed decision-making, such as refactoring and software modernization. Machine learning techniques have been used to detect design decisions in natural language discussions; however, their effectiveness is limited by the scarcity of labeled data and the high cost of annotation. Prior work adopted cross-domain strategies with traditional classifiers, training on one domain and testing on another. Despite their success, transformer-based models, which often outperform traditional methods, remain largely unexplored in this setting. The goal of this work is to investigate the performance of transformer-based models (i.e., BERT, RoBERTa, XLNet, LaMini-Flan-T5-77M, and ChatGPT-4o-mini) for detecting design-related discussions. To this end, we conduct a conceptual replication of prior cross-domain studies while extending them with modern transformer architectures and addressing methodological issues in earlier work. The models were fine-tuned on Stack Overflow and evaluated on GitHub artifacts (i.e., pull requests, issues, and commits). BERT and RoBERTa show strong recall across domains, while XLNet achieves higher precision but lower recall. ChatGPT-4o-mini yields the highest recall and competitive overall performance, whereas LaMini-Flan-T5-77M provides a lightweight alternative with stronger precision but less balanced performance. We also evaluated similar-word injection for data augmentation, but unlike prior findings, it did not yield meaningful improvements. Overall, these results highlight both the opportunities and trade-offs of using modern language models for detecting design discussion.

</details>


### [3] [SQL-Commenter: Aligning Large Language Models for SQL Comment Generation with Direct Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18606)
*Lei Yu,Peng Wang,Jingyuan Zhang,Xin Wang,Jia Xu,Li Yang,Changzhi Deng,Jiajia Ma,Fengjun Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SQL-Commenter是一种基于LLaMA-3.1-8B的SQL注释自动生成方法，通过构建复杂SQL查询数据集、持续预训练、监督微调和直接偏好优化(DPO)来提升LLM对SQL语义的理解，在Spider和Bird基准上显著优于现有最佳基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SQL查询理解困难，复杂语法、多样连接类型和深度嵌套导致代码可读性差；现有方法受限于数据集不足和LLM对SQL语义理解不足，即使经过持续预训练和监督微调仍难以准确生成注释。

Method: 1)构建包含复杂SQL查询及专家验证注释的综合数据集；2)在大型SQL语料上进行持续预训练以增强LLM的句法和语义理解；3)进行监督微调；4)引入基于人类反馈的直接偏好优化(DPO)，使用偏好损失函数优化细粒度语义学习和上下文相关质量评估。

Result: 在Spider和Bird基准上评估，SQL-Commenter显著优于SOTA基线；相比最强基线Qwen3-14B，平均提升BLEU-4 9.29%、METEOR 4.99%、ROUGE-L 13.23%。

Conclusion: 人类评估表明SQL-Commenter生成的注释在正确性、完整性和自然性方面表现优异，该方法有效解决了自动SQL注释生成中语义理解不足的问题。

Abstract: SQL query comprehension is a significant challenge due to complex syntax, diverse join types, and deep nesting. Many queries lack adequate comments, severely hindering code readability, maintainability, and knowledge transfer. Automated SQL comment generation faces two main challenges: limited datasets that inadequately represent complex real-world queries, and Large Language Models' (LLMs) insufficient understanding of SQL-specific semantics. Our empirical analysis shows that even after continual pre-training and supervised fine-tuning, LLMs struggle with complex SQL semantics, yielding inaccurate comments. To address this, we propose SQL-Commenter, an advanced method based on LLaMA-3.1-8B. We first construct a comprehensive dataset of complex SQL queries with expert-verified comments. Next, we perform continual pre-training on a large SQL corpus to enhance the LLM's syntax and semantic understanding, followed by supervised fine-tuning. Finally, we introduce Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) using human feedback. SQL-Commenter utilizes a preference-based loss function to favor preferred outputs, enhancing fine-grained semantic learning and context-dependent quality assessment. Evaluated on the Spider and Bird benchmarks, SQL-Commenter significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. On average, it surpasses the strongest baseline (Qwen3-14B) by 9.29, 4.99, and 13.23 percentage points on BLEU-4, METEOR, and ROUGE-L, respectively. Moreover, human evaluation demonstrates the superior quality of comments generated by SQL-Commenter in terms of correctness, completeness, and naturalness.

</details>


### [4] [Green Architectural Tactics in ML-enabled Systems: An LLM-based Repository Mining Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18734)
*Vincenzo De Martino,Silverio Martínez-Fernández,Fabio Palomba*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过挖掘205个GitHub开源ML项目的代码仓库，研究现有绿色AI实践的采用情况，并利用大语言模型识别出9个先前未文档化的可持续实践，为减少ML系统的环境影响提供实证基础和实践指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器学习和人工智能技术的广泛应用，其开发和部署过程中的计算资源消耗带来了严重的环境问题。Green AI旨在在不影响准确性的前提下提升效率。尽管已有研究提出了可持续实践的目录，但对这些实践在实际中的采用情况以及是否存在其他未文档化实践的理解仍然有限。

Method: 采用挖掘软件仓库（Mining Software Repository）方法，研究了GitHub上的205个开源ML项目。设计了一种基于大语言模型（LLM）的新型机制，能够从代码仓库中识别已知和新的可持续实践。

Result: 研究结果证实了文献中报道的绿色战术在实践中确实被采用，尽管采用率存在差异。此外，基于LLM的方法揭示了9个先前未文档化的可持续实践，每个实践都配有代码示例以帮助采用和集成。

Conclusion: 为寻求减少ML系统环境影响的从业者提供了见解，并为未来自动化检测和采用可持续实践的研究奠定了基础。研究强调了将理论可持续实践转化为实际代码实现的重要性。

Abstract: Context: The increasing adoption of machine learning (ML) and artificial intelligence (AI) technologies raises growing concerns about their environmental sustainability. Developing and deploying ML-enabled systems is computationally intensive, particularly during training and inference. Green AI has emerged to address these issues by promoting efficiency without sacrificing accuracy. While prior research has proposed catalogs of sustainable practices (i.e., green tactics), there remains limited understanding of their adoption in practice and whether additional, undocumented tactics exist. Objective: This study aims to investigate the extent to which existing sustainable practices are implemented in real-world ML-enabled systems and to identify previously undocumented practices that support environmental sustainability. Method: We conduct a mining software repository study on 205 open-source ML projects on GitHub. To support our analysis, we design a novel mechanism based on large language models (LLMs) capable of identifying both known and new sustainable practices from code repositories. Results: Our findings confirm that green tactics reported in the literature are used in practice, although adoption rates vary. Furthermore, our LLM-based approach reveals nine previously undocumented sustainable practices. Each tactic is supported with code examples to aid adoption and integration. Conclusions: We finally provide insights for practitioners seeking to reduce the environmental impact of ML-enabled systems and offer a foundation for future research in automating the detection and adoption of sustainable practices.

</details>


### [5] [SpaceTime Programming: Live and Omniscient Exploration of Code and Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18735)
*Jean-Baptiste Döderlein,Djamel Eddine Khelladi,Mathieu Acher,Benoit Combemale*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出SpaceTime Programming（时空编程）范式，统一了探索式编程、实时编程和全知调试三种范式，通过追踪机制同时捕获执行状态和代码变更，使开发者能在代码变体空间和执行流程时间两个维度上探索程序。实现了Python库SpaceTimePy，并在全知调试器和Flappy Bird游戏开发工具中应用，在5个真实Python项目中测试套件的性能开销为35%-150%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编程环境将静态代码与动态执行分离，开发者需要在编写代码和观察执行之间切换，且现有工具难以理解代码变更与运行时行为的关系。虽然存在探索式编程、实时编程、全知调试等范式来弥补这一 gap，但现有方案往往只关注特定方面，缺乏集成的多能力环境。

Method: 核心机制是trace追踪，不仅捕获执行状态，还捕获相应的代码变更，从而支持在空间（代码变体）和时间（执行流程）两个维度探索程序。实现了一个Python库SpaceTimePy。

Result: 实现了SpaceTimePy库，应用于两个场景：1）实时全知调试器；2）Pygame游戏开发工具（Flappy Bird类游戏）。在5个真实Python项目上评估，测试套件性能开销范围为35%-150%。

Conclusion: SpaceTime Programming成功统一了探索式编程、实时编程和全知调试三种范式，创建了一个能在代码空间和时间维度同时探索程序的编程模型，证明了该方法的可行性和实用性。

Abstract: Programming environments typically separate the world of static code from the dynamic execution of programs. Developers must switch between writing code and observing its execution, often with limited tools to understand the relationship between code changes and runtime behavior. Several paradigms and approaches exist to bridge this gap, including exploratory programming for comparing code variants, live programming for immediate feedback, and omniscient debugging for exploring execution history. However, existing solutions tend to focus on specific aspects and one specific paradigm rather than providing a fully integrated environment with multiple capabilities. This paper introduces \spacetime Programming, a novel approach that unifies these paradigms to create a programming model for exploring both code modifications and execution flow. At the core of our approach is a trace mechanism that captures not only execution state but also the corresponding code changes, enabling developers to explore programs in both space (code variants) and time (execution flow). As a proof of concept, we implemented a Python library supporting SpaceTime Programming and applied it in two contexts: a live omniscient debugger and a Pygame game development tool, showcased through a Flappy Bird-like game. We further evaluated SpaceTimePy on five real-world Python projects, finding performance overhead ranging from 35% to 150% on test suites.

</details>


### [6] [Measuring and Exploiting Confirmation Bias in LLM-Assisted Security Code Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18740)
*Dimitris Mitropoulos,Nikolaos Alexopoulos,Georgios Alexopoulos,Diomidis Spinellis*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Security code reviews increasingly rely on systems integrating Large Language Models (LLMs), ranging from interactive assistants to autonomous agents in CI/CD pipelines. We study whether confirmation bias (i.e., the tendency to favor interpretations that align with prior expectations) affects LLM-based vulnerability detection, and whether this failure mode can be exploited in software supply-chain attacks. We conduct two complementary studies.
  Study 1 quantifies confirmation bias through controlled experiments on 250 CVE vulnerability/patch pairs evaluated across four state-of-the-art models under five framing conditions for the review prompt. Framing a change as bug-free reduces vulnerability detection rates by 16-93%, with strongly asymmetric effects: false negatives increase sharply while false positive rates change little. Bias effects vary by vulnerability type, with injection flaws being more susceptible to them than memory corruption bugs.
  Study 2 evaluates exploitability in practice mimicking adversarial pull requests that reintroduce known vulnerabilities while framed as security improvements or urgent functionality fixes via their pull request metadata. Adversarial framing succeeds in 35% of cases against GitHub Copilot (interactive assistant) under one-shot attacks and in 88% of cases against Claude Code (autonomous agent) in real project configurations where adversaries can iteratively refine their framing to increase attack success. Debiasing via metadata redaction and explicit instructions restores detection in all interactive cases and 94% of autonomous cases. Our results show that confirmation bias poses a weakness in LLM-based code review, with implications on how AI-assisted development tools are deployed.

</details>

### [7] [From Servers to Sites: Compositional Power Trace Generation of LLM Inference for Infrastructure Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18383)
*Grant Wilkins,Fiodar Kazhamiaka,Ram Rajagopal*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个针对LLM推理工作负载的数据中心功耗建模与轨迹生成框架，能够在不同空间尺度（GPU服务器、机架、行、设施）和时间粒度下合成功耗曲线，解决了传统模型无法捕捉GPU在prefill、decode和idle状态间快速切换的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据中心功耗模型无法有效捕捉LLM推理工作负载的特殊性——GPU在计算密集的prefill、低功耗的decode和idle状态之间快速切换，且设施需求取决于这些状态在多个设备上的演化与同步特性。运营商需要细粒度轨迹进行资源调配，而公用事业公司需要场地级负载曲线进行容量规划。

Method: 提出一种组合式功耗表示方法，将LLM推理功耗分解为两个组件：1）工作负载驱动的运行状态转换；2）配置特定的功耗分布。基于此开发了一个轨迹生成框架，从测量轨迹中学习，为新的流量条件和服务配置合成功耗曲线。这些轨迹可从GPU服务器聚合到机架、行和设施级别的负载曲线。

Result: 在多个LLM、张量并行设置和GPU代际的测试中，该框架在大多数配置下实现了低于5%的中位绝对能量误差，同时保留了时间自相关结构。

Conclusion: 生成的轨迹支持超额订阅、功率调制和面向公用事业的负载特性分析等下游分析，使基础设施评估能够超越标称值假设和静态轨迹重放，为数据中心和电网的协同规划提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Datacenter operators and electrical utilities rely on power traces at different spatiotemporal scales. Operators use fine-grained traces for provisioning, facility management, and scheduling, while utilities use site-level load profiles for capacity and interconnection planning. Existing datacenter power models do not capture LLM inference workloads, in which GPUs shift rapidly among compute-intensive prefill, lower-power decode, and idle states, and facility demand depends on how these states evolve and synchronize across many devices. We show that LLM inference power can be represented compositionally through two components: workload-driven transitions among operating states and configuration-specific power distributions within those states. Building on this observation, we develop a trace-generation framework that learns from measured traces and synthesizes power profiles for new traffic conditions and serving configurations. These traces aggregate from GPU servers to rack-, row-, and facility-scale load profiles at the temporal granularity required by the study.
  Across multiple LLMs, tensor-parallel settings, and GPU generations, our framework achieves median absolute energy error below 5% for most configurations while preserving temporal autocorrelation structure. The resulting traces support downstream analyses including oversubscription, power modulation, and utility-facing load characterization, enabling infrastructure evaluations that flat nameplate assumptions and static trace replay cannot support.

</details>


### [8] [High-Performance Portable GPU Primitives for Arbitrary Types and Operators in Julia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18695)
*Emmanuel Pilliat*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: KernelForge.jl是一个Julia库，通过两层可移植架构在GPU上实现scan、mapreduce和矩阵向量原语，在NVIDIA A40和AMD MI300X上达到或超过CUB和cuBLAS的性能，证明可移植JIT编译抽象可以实现供应商级吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的便携式GPU框架（如Kokkos和RAJA）在基础并行原语上相对于供应商优化的库会产生可测量的开销，需要一种能够在保持通用性的同时达到供应商级性能的解决方案。

Method: 提出两层可移植架构：1) KernelIntrinsics.jl提供后端无关的warp级shuffle、内存栅栏和向量化内存访问抽象；2) KernelForge.jl基于这些接口构建高性能算法。

Result: 在NVIDIA A40上，KernelForge.jl的scan和mapreduce与CUB内核执行时间相当或更优；在NVIDIA A40和AMD MI300X上，矩阵向量操作与cuBLAS吞吐量相当。

Conclusion: 证明可移植的JIT编译抽象可以在不牺牲通用性的情况下实现供应商级吞吐量。

Abstract: Portable GPU frameworks such as Kokkos and RAJA reduce the burden of cross-architecture development but typically incur measurable overhead on fundamental parallel primitives relative to vendor-optimized libraries. We present KernelForge.jl, a Julia library that implements scan, mapreduce, and matrix-vector primitives through a two-layer portable architecture: KernelIntrinsics.jl provides backend-agnostic abstractions for warp-level shuffles, memory fences, and vectorized memory access, while KernelForge.jl builds high-performance algorithms exclusively on top of these interfaces. Evaluated on an NVIDIA A40 and an AMD MI300X, KernelForge.jl matches or exceeds CUB kernel execution time on scan and mapreduce on the A40, and matches cuBLAS throughput on matrix-vector operations across most tested configurations-demonstrating, as a proof of concept, that portable JIT-compiled abstractions can achieve vendor-level throughput without sacrificing generality.

</details>


### [9] [Literature Study on Operational Data Analytics Frameworks in Large-scale Computing Infrastructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19016)
*Shekhar Suman,Xiaoyu Chu,Alexandru Iosup*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于高性能计算(HPC)系统中运维数据分析(ODA)的文献综述研究，旨在提出一个更全面的ODA框架来应对大规模计算基础设施的管理和可持续性挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着HPC系统规模和复杂性的不断提升(到2025年每年产生泽字节数据)，系统可管理性和可持续性问题日益突出，需要通过细粒度监控和ODA能力来优化系统效率。

Method: 采用文献综述方法，列举了支持ODA能力的大规模计算基础设施基本要素，研究了流行于HPC环境中的各种ODA框架，并基于Sherif Sak et al提出的分布式图处理生态系统层次模型和Netti et al的ODA框架，提出了更全面的ODA框架。

Result: 提出了一个与大规模图处理分布式生态系统各层次相匹配的 holistic ODA框架，并与现有先进框架进行了对比以凸显其创新性，同时展示了实施先进ODA框架所带来的显著运维效率提升。

Conclusion: 通过文献综述和框架对比，突出了所提ODA框架的创新性，旨在引起学术界对这一领域的更多关注，并展望了该领域的未来研究方向。

Abstract: By 2025, there are zettabytes of data generated every year. The size and complexity of modern large-scale computing infrastructures like High-Performance Computing (HPC) systems continue to evolve and become complex, leaving us wondering about their manageability and sustainability concerns. Because of this reason, those complex systems are provided with fine-grained monitoring and Operational Data Analytics (ODA) capabilities to optimise their efficiency. In this literature study, we list the fundamental pillars of the large-scale computing infrastructures which enable its ODA capabilities, and conduct a study of the popular ODA frameworks operating in various such environments (predominantly HPC). Based on that, we propose a more holistic ODA framework matching the various layers of a large-scale graph-processing distributed ecosystem proposed by Sherif Sak et al, that extends the ODA functionalities presented in an existing novel ODA framework proposed by Netti et al. We compare the holistic ODA framework proposed by us to some of the state-of-the-art frameworks that we study as part of this literature to highlight the novelty, which would hopefully draw more attention to perform extensive research in this field. As part of creating awareness, we highlight the significant operational efficiencies observed as a result of the implementation of the state-of-the-art ODA frameworks to make the study appear beneficial for the readers, and lastly, discuss the trending research work ongoing in this field.

</details>


### [10] [Why Synchronized Time is a Fiction: Daylight Saving Time, Leap Seconds, and the Guillotine Sharpened for Nothing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19099)
*Paul Borrill*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文认为"存在单一全球时间"这一假设构成了赖尔(1949)意义上的范畴错误。相对论禁止绝对同时性，光速单向是约定而非测量，量子实验显示自然允许无明确时间序列的相关性。这种范畴错误在分布式计算中表现为FITO假设，影响了Lamport逻辑时钟、FLP不可能定理和CAP定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文旨在批判关于时间同步的基础假设，指出"单一全球时间"的概念本身是一个范畴错误，需要重新审视时间在物理学和计算系统中的地位。

Method: 本文采用跨学科分析方法，结合:
1. 物理学论证: 相对论(特殊/广义)证明绝对同时性不存在
2. 哲学分析: 引用赖尔的范畴错误概念
3. 实证依据: 光速单向性的约定性、因果序实验
4. 计算理论追踪: 分布式系统中的FITO假设及其影响

Result: 本文揭示:
- 绝对同时性被相对论禁止
- 单向光速是定义而非测量结果
- 量子实验显示无明确时间序列的相关性
- FITO假设影响Lamport时钟、FLP不可能定理和CAP定理
- 夏令时/闰秒不是时间修正而是修正约定

Conclusion: 单一全球时间的存在假设是一个范畴错误。夏令时和闰秒实际上是在修正"同步的断头台"——为一个不存在的事物做准备。时间同步基础设施可能需要根本性的概念重构。

Abstract: Civilization maintains an elaborate infrastructure devoted to the maintenance of synchronized time. Governments mandate daylight saving time. Standards bodies insert leap seconds into Coordinated Universal Time. Engineers debate leap milliseconds and leap nanoseconds. The Global Positioning System applies relativistic corrections at the nanosecond level. All of these adjustments attempt to preserve an assumption: that a single global time exists and that clocks can be made to agree upon it.
  This paper argues that this assumption constitutes a category mistake in the sense of Ryle (1949). We show that special and general relativity prohibit absolute simultaneity, that the one-way speed of light is conventionally defined rather than measured, and that recent experiments on indefinite causal order demonstrate nature admits correlations with no well-defined temporal sequence. We trace the consequences of this category mistake through distributed computing, where it manifests as the Forward-In-Time-Only (FITO) assumption that underlies Lamport's logical clocks (1978), the impossibility results of Fischer-Lynch-Paterson (1985), and the CAP theorem (2000). From this perspective, daylight saving time and leap seconds are not corrections to time but corrections to conventions -- they sharpen the guillotine of synchronization in preparation for executing something that does not exist.

</details>

### [11] [MemArchitect: A Policy Driven Memory Governance Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18330)
*Lingavasan Suresh Kumar,Yang Ba,Rong Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MemArchitect是一个治理层框架，用于解决持久化LLM智能体中内存管理的关键治理缺口。它将内存生命周期管理与模型权重解耦，通过显式规则策略（记忆衰减、冲突解决、隐私控制）来管理记忆。实验表明，受治理的记忆在智能体设置中始终优于无管理的记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决标准RAG框架中内存作为被动存储的局限性：无法解决矛盾信息、缺乏隐私保护机制、无法防止过时信息（"僵尸记忆"）污染上下文窗口。这些问题在持久化LLM智能体中构成关键治理缺口。

Method: MemArchitect作为独立于模型权重的治理层，通过显式规则策略强制执行内存生命周期管理，包括：1) 记忆衰减机制；2) 冲突解决策略；3) 隐私控制机制。

Result: 受治理的记忆在智能体设置中始终优于无管理的记忆，证明了结构化内存治理对可靠和安全自主系统的必要性。

Conclusion: 结构化内存治理对于构建可靠和安全的自主系统至关重要。该研究强调了将内存管理从被动存储转变为主动治理的范式转变必要性。

Abstract: Persistent Large Language Model (LLM) agents expose a critical governance gap in memory management. Standard Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) frameworks treat memory as passive storage, lacking mechanisms to resolve contradictions, enforce privacy, or prevent outdated information ("zombie memories") from contaminating the context window.
  We introduce MemArchitect, a governance layer that decouples memory lifecycle management from model weights. MemArchitect enforces explicit, rule-based policies, including memory decay, conflict resolution, and privacy controls.
  We demonstrate that governed memory consistently outperforms unmanaged memory in agentic settings, highlighting the necessity of structured memory governance for reliable and safe autonomous systems.

</details>


### [12] [DEAF: A Benchmark for Diagnostic Evaluation of Acoustic Faithfulness in Audio Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18048)
*Jiaqi Xiong,Yunjia Qi,Qi Cao,Yu Zheng,Weisheng Xu,Ziteng Wang,Ruofan Liao,Yutong Zhang,Sichen Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DEAF基准测试用于诊断音频多模态大语言模型是否真正处理声学信号还是依赖文本语义推理。评估7个模型发现存在文本主导现象：模型对声学变化敏感，但预测主要由文本输入驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前音频多模态大语言模型(Audio MLLMs)在语音基准测试中表现优异，但尚不清楚这些模型是真正处理声学信号还是仅依赖文本语义推理。需要系统性地研究这一关键问题。

Method: 构建DEAF基准测试，包含超过2700个冲突刺激样本，涵盖三个声学维度：情感韵律、背景声音和说话人身份。设计受控的多级评估框架，逐步增加文本影响力，从内容语义冲突到误导性提示的组合，以分离内容驱动偏差和提示诱导的谄媚行为。

Result: 对7个Audio MLLMs的评估揭示了一致的文本主导模式：模型对声学变化敏感，但预测主要由文本输入驱动，显示出标准语音基准测试的高性能与真正的声学理解之间存在差距。

Conclusion: Audio MLLMs在标准基准上表现良好，但缺乏真正的声学理解能力，主要依赖文本线索进行预测。这一发现呼吁未来的研究应关注模型对声学信息的真实处理能力。

Abstract: Recent Audio Multimodal Large Language Models (Audio MLLMs) demonstrate impressive performance on speech benchmarks, yet it remains unclear whether these models genuinely process acoustic signals or rely on text-based semantic inference. To systematically study this question, we introduce DEAF (Diagnostic Evaluation of Acoustic Faithfulness), a benchmark of over 2,700 conflict stimuli spanning three acoustic dimensions: emotional prosody, background sounds, and speaker identity. Then, we design a controlled multi-level evaluation framework that progressively increases textual influence, ranging from semantic conflicts in the content to misleading prompts and their combination, allowing us to disentangle content-driven bias from prompt-induced sycophancy. We further introduce diagnostic metrics to quantify model reliance on textual cues over acoustic signals. Our evaluation of seven Audio MLLMs reveals a consistent pattern of text dominance: models are sensitive to acoustic variations, yet predictions are predominantly driven by textual inputs, revealing a gap between high performance on standard speech benchmarks and genuine acoustic understanding.

</details>


### [13] [Continually self-improving AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18073)
*Zitong Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出三个研究方向，旨在突破现代语言模型的三大固有局限：数据效率依赖、人类数据依赖和人类设计的训练范式依赖，通过合成数据、自生成数据和算法搜索空间扩展，实现持续自我改进的AI系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代语言模型的能力受到三个方面的根本限制：1) 从小规模专业语料库获取新知识的数据效率低下；2) 训练依赖有限的人类历史数据；3) 训练管道受限于人类研究者能发现和探索的算法空间。这些限制阻碍了AI系统的持续自我改进能力。

Method: 提出三个解决方案：1) 合成数据方法：将小规模语料库多样化并增强为丰富的知识表示，使模型能够从有限源材料有效更新参数；2) 自我生成合成数据：在固定人类数据量下，模型自生成合成数据来引导基础预训练能力，无需从现成指令微调LM蒸馏；3) 扩展算法搜索空间：通过在测试时扩展搜索算法空间，使AI能够探索比人类研究者手动探索更大的学习算法配置空间。

Result: 三项研究分别实现了：1) 突破知识获取的数据效率障碍；2) 减少对人类数据的依赖，在固定数据量下实现自我 bootstrapping；3) 超越人类设计的训练范式。

Conclusion: 通过合成数据、自生成数据和算法搜索扩展三个方向，为创建持续自我改进的AI系统迈出了小而重要的步伐，逐步打破了对人类 creator、数据和训练范式的依赖。

Abstract: Modern language model-based AI systems are remarkably powerful, yet their capabilities remain fundamentally capped by their human creators in three key ways. First, although a model's weights can be updated via fine-tuning, acquiring new knowledge from small, specialized corpora after pretraining remains highly data-inefficient. Second, the training of these systems relies heavily on finite, human-generated data from across history. Third, the pipelines used to train AI models are confined by the algorithms that human researchers can discover and explore. This thesis takes a small step toward overcoming these inherent limitations, presenting three chapters aimed at breaking these dependencies to create continually self-improving AI. First, to overcome this data-efficiency barrier in knowledge acquisition, we propose a synthetic data approach that diversifies and amplifies small corpora into rich knowledge representations, enabling a model to effectively update its parameters from limited source material. Second, to reduce reliance on human data, we show that given a fixed amount of such data, the model can self-generate synthetic data to bootstrap its fundamental pretraining capabilities without distillation from any off-the-shelf, instruction-tuned LM. Finally, to transcend human-engineered training paradigms, we demonstrate that by scaling search during test time over the space of algorithms, AI can search over a larger space of learning algorithm configurations than human researchers can explore manually.

</details>


### [14] [Multi-Trait Subspace Steering to Reveal the Dark Side of Human-AI Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18085)
*Xin Wei Chia,Swee Liang Wong,Jonathan Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对人类-AI交互中产生的负面心理后果问题,提出了Multi-Traits Subspace Steering(MultiTraitsss)框架,通过危机相关特征和子空间 steering 技术生成具有累积有害行为模式的"暗模型",并基于此提出保护措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型(LLM)被广泛应用于提供指导、情感支持甚至非正式心理治疗,人类-AI交互中出现了负面心理后果的案例,包括心理健康危机甚至用户伤害。由于这类有害交互通常在持续互动中逐渐发展,需要广泛的对话上下文,而受控环境下难以模拟,因此研究其机制面临重大方法论挑战。

Method: 提出了Multi-Traits Subspace Steering(MultiTraitsss)框架,该框架利用危机相关特征(crisis-associated traits)和新型子空间steering框架来生成具有累积有害行为模式的Dark模型。进行了单轮和多轮评估。

Result: 单轮和多轮评估表明,所提出的暗模型持续产生有害交互和负面结果。

Conclusion: 成功构建了可模拟有害人类-AI交互的暗模型,并基于此提出了减少人类-AI交互中有害后果的保护措施。

Abstract: Recent incidents have highlighted alarming cases where human-AI interactions led to negative psychological outcomes, including mental health crises and even user harm. As LLMs serve as sources of guidance, emotional support, and even informal therapy, these risks are poised to escalate. However, studying the mechanisms underlying harmful human-AI interactions presents significant methodological challenges, where organic harmful interactions typically develop over sustained engagement, requiring extensive conversational context that are difficult to simulate in controlled settings. To address this gap, we developed a Multi-Trait Subspace Steering (MultiTraitsss) framework that leverages established crisis-associated traits and novel subspace steering framework to generate Dark models that exhibits cumulative harmful behavioral patterns. Single-turn and multi-turn evaluations show that our dark models consistently produce harmful interaction and outcomes. Using our Dark models, we propose protective measure to reduce harmful outcomes in Human-AI interactions.

</details>


### [15] [Adaptive Domain Models: Bayesian Evolution, Warm Rotation, and Principled Training for Geometric and Neuromorphic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18104)
*Houston Haynes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种替代性AI训练架构，突破传统基于IEEE-754算术和反向模式自动微分的训练范式。通过结合维度类型系统与确定性内存管理框架、程序超图（几何代数计算中的等级保持）以及b-posit 2026标准，该架构实现了深度无关的训练内存（约2倍推理内存）、精确梯度累积和等级保持的权重更新，并引入贝叶斯蒸馏和warm rotation机制支持领域特定AI系统的持续适应与安全部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI训练基础设施基于IEEE-754算术的反向模式自动微分，存在三方面核心问题：1) 训练相对推理的内存开销过大；2) 优化器复杂度高；3) 训练过程中几何属性的结构性退化。这些问题源于传统算术基底的固有局限性，亟需一种新的训练架构来克服这些根本性约束。

Method: 本文提出三种核心技术框架的组合：1) 维度类型系统与确定性内存管理框架——建立栈式梯度分配和精确quire累积作为设计时可验证属性；2) 程序超图——将几何代数计算中的等级保持确立为类型级不变量；3) b-posit 2026标准——使posi算术在传统推理硬件上可行。在此基础上引入贝叶斯蒸馏机制从通用模型提取潜在先验结构解决数据稀缺问题，以及warm rotation模式实现模型更新时服务不中断的部署过渡。

Result: 该架构实现了：1) 深度无关的训练内存，限制在推理内存的约2倍；2) 等级保持的权重更新；3) 精确梯度累积；4) 统一适用于损失函数优化模型和脉冲时序依赖的神经形态模型；5) 可验证的结构正确性（通过PHG证书和签名版本记录）；6) 相比通用模型更小更精确的领域特定AI系统。

Conclusion: 本文所提出的ADM训练架构成功突破了传统反向模式自动微分的内存和精度限制，通过几何代数与精确算术的深度融合，为领域特定AI系统提供了持续适应、可验证正确性以及从现有模型初始化的能力，代表了AI训练基础设施的重要范式转变。

Abstract: Prevailing AI training infrastructure assumes reverse-mode automatic differentiation over IEEE-754 arithmetic. The memory overhead of training relative to inference, optimizer complexity, and structural degradation of geometric properties through training are consequences of this arithmetic substrate. This paper develops an alternative training architecture grounded in three prior results: the Dimensional Type System and Deterministic Memory Management framework [6], which establishes stack-eligible gradient allocation and exact quire accumulation as design-time verifiable properties; the Program Hypergraph [8], which establishes grade preservation through geometric algebra computations as a type-level invariant; and the b-posit 2026 standard [10], which makes posit arithmetic tractable across hardware targets conventionally considered inference-only. Their composition enables depth-independent training memory bounded to approximately twice the inference footprint, grade-preserving weight updates, and exact gradient accumulation, applicable uniformly to loss-function-optimized and spike-timing-dependent neuromorphic models. We introduce Bayesian distillation, a mechanism by which the latent prior structure of a general-purpose model is extracted through the ADM training regime, resolving the data-scarcity bootstrapping problem for domain-specific training. For deployment, we introduce warm rotation, an operational pattern in which an updated model transitions into an active inference pathway without service interruption, with structural correctness formalized through PHG certificates and signed version records. The result is a class of domain-specific AI systems that are smaller and more precise than general-purpose models, continuously adaptive, verifiably correct with respect to the physical structure of their domains, and initializable from existing models.

</details>


### [16] [Don't Vibe Code, Do Skele-Code: Interactive No-Code Notebooks for Subject Matter Experts to Build Lower-Cost Agentic Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18122)
*Sriram Gopalakrishnan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Skele-Code是一个面向非技术用户的自然语言和图式工作流构建界面，通过增量式、交互式笔记本风格开发，支持将每个步骤转换为代码。AI智能体仅用于代码生成和错误恢复，不参与编排或任务执行，这种代码优先的方法可以降低token成本，并生成模块化、可扩展、可共享的工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在降低AI工作流构建的门槛，使非技术用户也能创建和定制AI工作流，同时解决多智能体系统方法带来的高token成本问题。

Method: 采用自然语言和图式界面结合的方式，支持增量式交互开发；智能体仅负责代码生成和错误恢复，不参与工作流的编排和执行；通过上下文工程(context-engineering)优化token使用。

Result: 生成的代码优先工作流具有模块化、可扩展性和可共享性；可作为智能体的技能或其他工作流的步骤使用；相比多智能体系统方法能降低token成本。

Conclusion: Skele-Code提供了一种更高效、更低成本的工作流构建范式，通过代码优先的方法和上下文工程，使非技术用户能够更容易地构建AI工作流，同时保持工作流的模块化和可复用性。

Abstract: Skele-Code is a natural-language and graph-based interface for building workflows with AI agents, designed especially for less or non-technical users. It supports incremental, interactive notebook-style development, and each step is converted to code with a required set of functions and behavior to enable incremental building of workflows. Agents are invoked only for code generation and error recovery, not orchestration or task execution. This agent-supported, but code-first approach to workflows, along with the context-engineering used in Skele-Code, can help reduce token costs compared to the multi-agent system approach to executing workflows. Skele-Code produces modular, easily extensible, and shareable workflows. The generated workflows can also be used as skills by agents, or as steps in other workflows.

</details>


### [17] [Efficient Dense Crowd Trajectory Prediction Via Dynamic Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18166)
*Antonius Bima Murti Wijaya,Paul Henderson,Marwa Mahmoud*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于聚类的群体轨迹预测方法，通过将具有相似属性的个体进行分组汇总，实现更快的处理速度和更低的内存占用。该方法可作为即插即用的模块与现有轨迹预测器结合使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖手动标注数据，忽略了密集人群场景，且存在追踪输出噪声大、不准确的问题，导致计算成本高昂，难以满足实际应用需求。

Method: 提出一种聚类方法，根据个体相似属性随时间变化进行分组，通过精确的群体汇总实现快速执行。该方法作为即插即用模块，可替代现有预测器中的行人输入，使用群体质心作为输入。在多个具有挑战性的密集人群场景中进行评估。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在保持准确性的前提下，实现了更快的处理速度和更低的内存占用。

Conclusion: 基于聚类的群体轨迹预测方法能够有效解决密集人群场景下的轨迹预测效率问题，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Crowd trajectory prediction plays a crucial role in public safety and management, where it can help prevent disasters such as stampedes. Recent works address the problem by predicting individual trajectories and considering surrounding objects based on manually annotated data. However, these approaches tend to overlook dense crowd scenarios, where the challenges of automation become more pronounced due to the massiveness, noisiness, and inaccuracy of the tracking outputs, resulting in high computational costs. To address these challenges, we propose and extensively evaluate a novel cluster-based approach that groups individuals based on similar attributes over time, enabling faster execution through accurate group summarisation. Our plug-and-play method can be combined with existing trajectory predictors by using our output centroid in place of their pedestrian input. We evaluate our proposed method on several challenging dense crowd scenes. We demonstrated that our approach leads to faster processing and lower memory usage when compared with state-of-the-art methods, while maintaining the accuracy

</details>


### [18] [TeachingCoach: A Fine-Tuned Scaffolding Chatbot for Instructional Guidance to Instructors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18189)
*Isabel Molnar,Peiyu Li,Si Chen,Sugana Chawla,James Lang,Ronald Metoyer,Ting Hua,Nitesh V. Chawla*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TeachingCoach是一款基于教学法的聊天机器人，通过从教育资源中提取教学规则并使用合成对话微调专门的语言模型，为高等教育教师提供实时会话指导。评估表明其表现优于GPT-4o mini基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高等教育教师缺乏及时且基于教学法的支持，现有的教学支持工具要么依赖通用聊天机器人建议，要么需要人工咨询（不可扩展）。

Method: 构建以数据为中心的pipeline：从教育资源中提取教学规则，使用合成对话生成微调专门的语言模型。进行专家评估和用户研究。

Result: TeachingCoach产生更清晰、更具反思性、更具响应性的指导；用户研究揭示了会话深度与交互效率之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 基于教学法的合成数据驱动聊天机器人可以改善教学支持，为未来教学聊天机器人系统提供可扩展的设计方法。

Abstract: Higher education instructors often lack timely and pedagogically grounded support, as scalable instructional guidance remains limited and existing tools rely on generic chatbot advice or non-scalable teaching center human-human consultations. We present TeachingCoach, a pedagogically grounded chatbot designed to support instructor professional development through real-time, conversational guidance. TeachingCoach is built on a data-centric pipeline that extracts pedagogical rules from educational resources and uses synthetic dialogue generation to fine-tune a specialized language model that guides instructors through problem identification, diagnosis, and strategy development. Expert evaluations show TeachingCoach produces clearer, more reflective, and more responsive guidance than a GPT-4o mini baseline, while a user study with higher education instructors highlights trade-offs between conversational depth and interaction efficiency. Together, these results demonstrate that pedagogically grounded, synthetic data driven chatbots can improve instructional support and offer a scalable design approach for future instructional chatbot systems.

</details>


### [19] [cuGenOpt: A GPU-Accelerated General-Purpose Metaheuristic Framework for Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19163)
*Yuyang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: cuGenOpt是一个GPU加速的通用元启发式框架，通过"一block演化一解"的CUDA架构、统一编码抽象、两级自适应算子选择和LLM建模助手等技术，同时解决了组合优化中通用性、性能和可用性的权衡问题，在多个基准问题上取得优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合优化问题在物流、调度和资源分配等领域广泛应用，但现有方法在通用性、性能和可用性之间存在根本性的权衡冲突。现有方法难以同时在这三个维度上取得良好表现。

Method: 提出cuGenOpt，一个GPU加速的通用元启发式框架。在引擎层面，采用"一个block演化一个解"的CUDA架构，支持统一编码抽象（排列、二进制、整数），包含两级自适应算子选择机制和硬件感知的资源管理。在可扩展性层面，提供用户自定义算子注册接口，允许领域专家注入问题特定的CUDA搜索算子。在可用性层面，JIT编译流水线将框架暴露为纯Python API，并基于LLM的建模助手将自然语言问题描述转换为可执行求解器代码。

Result: 在三个GPU架构（T4、V100、A800）上对五个主题问题套件的实验表明：cuGenOpt在通用性上比MIP求解器快几个数量级；在n≤150的实例上与专用求解器质量相当；TSP-442在30秒内达到4.73%的gap。12种问题类型跨越5种编码变体均求得最优解。框架级优化累计将pcb442的gap从36%降至4.73%，VRPTW吞吐量提升75-81%。

Conclusion: cuGenOpt成功实现了在通用性、性能和可用性三个维度上的同时优化，为组合优化问题提供了一个高效、可扩展且用户友好的GPU加速求解框架。

Abstract: Combinatorial optimization problems arise in logistics, scheduling, and resource allocation, yet existing approaches face a fundamental trade-off among generality, performance, and usability. We present cuGenOpt, a GPU-accelerated general-purpose metaheuristic framework that addresses all three dimensions simultaneously.
  At the engine level, cuGenOpt adopts a "one block evolves one solution" CUDA architecture with a unified encoding abstraction (permutation, binary, integer), a two-level adaptive operator selection mechanism, and hardware-aware resource management. At the extensibility level, a user-defined operator registration interface allows domain experts to inject problem-specific CUDA search operators. At the usability level, a JIT compilation pipeline exposes the framework as a pure-Python API, and an LLM-based modeling assistant converts natural-language problem descriptions into executable solver code.
  Experiments across five thematic suites on three GPU architectures (T4, V100, A800) show that cuGenOpt outperforms general MIP solvers by orders of magnitude, achieves competitive quality against specialized solvers on instances up to n=150, and attains 4.73% gap on TSP-442 within 30s. Twelve problem types spanning five encoding variants are solved to optimality. Framework-level optimizations cumulatively reduce pcb442 gap from 36% to 4.73% and boost VRPTW throughput by 75-81%.
  Code: https://github.com/L-yang-yang/cugenopt

</details>


### [20] [A Computationally Efficient Learning of Artificial Intelligence System Reliability Considering Error Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18201)
*Fenglian Pan,Yinwei Zhang,Yili Hong,Larry Head,Jian Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对智能城市中AI系统可靠性建模的挑战，提出利用自动驾驶物理仿真平台生成高质量数据，并开发了新的可靠性建模框架来表征跨阶段误差传播，使用计算高效的复合似然EM算法进行参数估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统在智慧城市中的应用日益普及，但其可靠性问题日益突出。AI系统通常通过相互连接的功能阶段序列运行，上游误差可能向下游传播，影响整体系统可靠性。准确量化这种误差传播对于AI系统可靠性建模至关重要，但面临数据稀缺、误差事件的互依性违反统计推断独立性假设、以及高频复杂 recurrent error事件难以追踪等挑战。

Method: 1) 利用基于物理的自动驾驶仿真平台和合理的误差注入器生成高质量AI系统可靠性数据；2) 开发新的可靠性建模框架，明确表征跨阶段误差传播；3) 采用计算高效、理论保证的复合似然期望最大化(EM)算法估计模型参数。

Result: 该方法在自动驾驶汽车感知系统可靠性建模中的应用表明，其具有较高的预测准确性和计算效率。

Conclusion: 通过物理仿真解决数据稀缺问题，结合跨阶段误差传播建模和高效参数估计算法，为AI系统可靠性分析提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI) systems are increasingly prominent in emerging smart cities, yet their reliability remains a critical concern. These systems typically operate through a sequence of interconnected functional stages, where upstream errors may propagate to downstream stages, ultimately affecting overall system reliability. Quantifying such error propagation is essential for accurate modeling of AI system reliability. However, this task is challenging due to: i) data availability: real-world AI system reliability data are often scarce and constrained by privacy concerns; ii) model validity: recurring error events across sequential stages are interdependent, violating the independence assumptions of statistical inference; and iii) computational complexity: AI systems process large volumes of high-speed data, resulting in frequent and complex recurrent error events that are difficult to track and analyze. To address these challenges, this paper leverages a physics-based autonomous vehicle simulation platform with a justifiable error injector to generate high-quality data for AI system reliability analysis. Building on this data, a new reliability modeling framework is developed to explicitly characterize error propagation across stages. Model parameters are estimated using a computationally efficient, theoretically guaranteed composite likelihood expectation - maximization algorithm. Its application to the reliability modeling for autonomous vehicle perception systems demonstrates its predictive accuracy and computational efficiency.

</details>


### [21] [Retrieval-Augmented LLM Agents: Learning to Learn from Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18272)
*Thomas Palmeira Ferraz,Romain Deffayet,Vassilina Nikoulina,Hervé Déjean,Stéphane Clinchant*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出结合微调和检索增强生成两种方法，通过LoRA监督微调+经验检索来提升LLM智能体在新任务上的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM智能体面临泛化到新任务的挑战：微调方法无法外推到新任务，检索增强方法性能常低于监督基线。

Method: 1) 建立基于LoRA的SFT训练配方；2) 详细分析经验检索的关键设计选择（存储、查询、轨迹选择）；3) 将经验检索集成到微调流程中。

Result: 该组合方法显著提升了对 unseen 任务的泛化能力，提供了可扩展的"学会学习"智能体构建框架。

Conclusion: 结合微调和检索增强的方案是构建泛化能力强的新一代智能体的有效途径。

Abstract: While large language models (LLMs) have advanced the development of general-purpose agents, achieving robust generalization to unseen tasks remains a significant challenge. Current approaches typically rely on either fine-tuning or training-free memory-augmented generation using retrieved experience; yet both have limitations: fine-tuning often fails to extrapolate to new tasks, while experience retrieval often underperforms compared to supervised baselines. In this work, we propose to combine these approaches and systematically study how to train retrieval-augmented LLM agents to effectively leverage retrieved trajectories in-context. First, we establish a robust supervised fine-tuning (SFT) recipe using LoRA that outperforms several state-of-the-art agent training pipelines. Second, we provide a detailed analysis of key design choices for experience retrieval, identifying optimal strategies for storage, querying, and trajectory selection. Finally, we propose a pipeline that integrates experience retrieval into the fine-tuning process. Our results demonstrate that this combined approach significantly improves generalization to unseen tasks, providing a scalable and effective framework for building agents that learn to learn from experience.

</details>


### [22] [EDM-ARS: A Domain-Specific Multi-Agent System for Automated Educational Data Mining Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18273)
*Chenguang Pan,Zhou Zhang,Weixuan Xiao,Chengyuan Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EDM-ARS是一个领域特定的多智能体管道系统,用于自动化教育数据挖掘(EDM)研究的端到端流程。该系统通过5个专门的大语言模型智能体(ProblemFormulator、DataEngineer、Analyst、Critic和Writer)在状态机协调器的管理下,实现从研究提示和数据集到完整LaTeX论文手稿的自动化生成,包括经过验证的机器学习分析和自动化方法论同行评审。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前教育数据挖掘研究流程存在效率低下的问题,研究者需要手动完成从问题定式化、数据处理、分析建模到论文写作的完整链条。现有自动化研究管道缺乏教育领域专业知识的嵌入,无法满足教育研究社区对标准化、可复现研究流程的需求。

Method: EDM-ARS采用多智能体架构,包含5个专门化的LLM驱动智能体,通过状态机协调器进行管理,支持修订循环、基于检查点的恢复和沙箱代码执行。系统包含三层数据注册设计来编码教育领域专业知识,定义了智能体间通信协议及错误处理和自纠正机制。作为框架的首个实例化,重点关注预测建模任务。

Result: 给定研究提示和数据集,EDM-ARS能够生成包含真实Semantic Scholar引用的完整LaTeX论文手稿,包含经过验证的机器学习分析和自动化方法论评审。系统已作为开源项目发布。

Conclusion: EDM-ARS展示了将教育领域专业知识嵌入自动化研究管道的可行性,为教育研究社区提供了标准化研究工具。当前局限包括单数据集范围和公式化论文输出。未来路线图包括因果推断、迁移学习、心理测量和多数据集泛化等方向的扩展。

Abstract: In this technical report, we present the Educational Data Mining Automated Research System (EDM-ARS), a domain-specific multi-agent pipeline that automates end-to-end educational data mining (EDM) research. We conceptualize EDM-ARS as a general framework for domain-aware automated research pipelines, where educational expertise is embedded into each stage of the research lifecycle. As a first instantiation of this framework, we focus on predictive modeling tasks. Within this scope, EDM-ARS orchestrates five specialized LLM-powered agents (ProblemFormulator, DataEngineer, Analyst, Critic, and Writer) through a state-machine coordinator that supports revision loops, checkpoint-based recovery, and sandboxed code execution. Given a research prompt and a dataset, EDM-ARS produces a complete LaTeX manuscript with real Semantic Scholar citations, validated machine learning analyses, and automated methodological peer review. We also provide a detailed description of the system architecture, the three-tier data registry design that encodes educational domain expertise, the specification of each agent, the inter-agent communication protocol, and mechanisms for error-handling and self-correction. Finally, we discuss current limitations, including single-dataset scope and formulaic paper output, and outline a phased roadmap toward causal inference, transfer learning, psychometric, and multi-dataset generalization. EDM-ARS is released as an open-source project to support the educational research community.

</details>


### [23] [CORE: Robust Out-of-Distribution Detection via Confidence and Orthogonal Residual Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18290)
*Jin Mo Yang,Hyung-Sin Kim,Saewoong Bahk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CORE方法通过解耦penultimate特征中的分类器对齐分量（置信度信号）和残差分量（成员信号），在五个架构和五个基准配置中实现竞争或最先进性能，在三个设置中排名第一，并获得最高的总体平均AUROC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前OOD检测方法缺乏一致性：基于logit的方法仅看到分类器的置信度信号，基于特征的方法在完整特征空间中测量成员资格但信号纠缠。不同方法在不同基准上表现不一致，缺乏一种能在各种架构和数据集上稳定工作的方法。

Method: CORE方法将penultimate特征分解为两个正交子空间：分类器对齐分量（编码置信度）和残差分量（分类器丢弃的部分）。对每个子空间独立评分，通过归一化求和组合两个分数。由于两个信号正交构建，失败模式近似独立。

Result: 在五个架构和五个基准配置中达到竞争或最先进性能；在三个设置中排名第一；实现最高总体平均AUROC；计算开销可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 通过解耦置信度信号和成员信号并利用其正交性，CORE克服了现有方法的结构局限性，实现了鲁棒的OOD检测，为不一致性问题提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Out-of-distribution (OOD) detection is essential for deploying deep learning models reliably, yet no single method performs consistently across architectures and datasets -- a scorer that leads on one benchmark often falters on another. We attribute this inconsistency to a shared structural limitation: logit-based methods see only the classifier's confidence signal, while feature-based methods attempt to measure membership in the training distribution but do so in the full feature space where confidence and membership are entangled, inheriting architecture-sensitive failure modes. We observe that penultimate features naturally decompose into two orthogonal subspaces: a classifier-aligned component encoding confidence, and a residual the classifier discards. We discover that this residual carries a class-specific directional signature for in-distribution data -- a membership signal invisible to logit-based methods and entangled with noise in feature-based methods. We propose CORE (COnfidence + REsidual), which disentangles the two signals by scoring each subspace independently and combines them via normalized summation. Because the two signals are orthogonal by construction, their failure modes are approximately independent, producing robust detection where either view alone is unreliable. CORE achieves competitive or state-of-the-art performance across five architectures and five benchmark configurations, ranking first in three of five settings and achieving the highest grand average AUROC with negligible computational overhead.

</details>


### [24] [The Validity Gap in Health AI Evaluation: A Cross-Sectional Analysis of Benchmark Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18294)
*Alvin Rajkomar,Pavan Sudarshan,Angela Lai,Lily Peng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 健康相关大型语言模型(LLM)的基准测试存在"有效性差距"，与真实临床需求存在显著 misalignment：缺乏原始临床数据、对弱势群体(儿童/老年人<11%)和慢性病管理(5.5%)的代表性不足，安全关键场景几乎缺失(自杀/自残<0.7%)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床试验依赖透明的纳入标准确保泛化性，但验证健康相关LLM的基准测试很少描述其包含的"患者"或"查询"人群。缺乏定义的人口组成， aggregate performance metrics可能无法代表模型临床使用的准备程度。

Method: 分析18,707个消费者健康查询横跨6个公共基准，使用LLM作为自动化编码工具应用标准化16字段分类法分析context、topic和intent。

Result: 发现结构性"有效性差距"：虽然基准从静态检索演变为交互式对话，但临床组成仍与真实世界需求不一致。42%语料库引用客观数据，但偏向于可穿戴设备信号(17.7%)；复杂诊断输入罕见，包括实验室值(5.2%)、影像(3.8%)、原始医疗记录(0.6%)。安全关键场景几乎缺失：自杀/自残查询<0.7%，慢性病管理仅5.5%。基准也忽视弱势群体(儿童/老年人<11%)和全球健康需求。

Conclusion: 评估基准与真实临床需求不一致，缺乏原始临床artifact、对弱势群体的充分代表性以及纵向慢性病护理场景。必须采用标准化的查询profiling——类似于临床试验报告——以使评估与临床实践的全面复杂性相一致。

Abstract: Background: Clinical trials rely on transparent inclusion criteria to ensure generalizability. In contrast, benchmarks validating health-related large language models (LLMs) rarely characterize the "patient" or "query" populations they contain. Without defined composition, aggregate performance metrics may misrepresent model readiness for clinical use.
  Methods: We analyzed 18,707 consumer health queries across six public benchmarks using LLMs as automated coding instruments to apply a standardized 16-field taxonomy profiling context, topic, and intent.
  Results: We identified a structural "validity gap." While benchmarks have evolved from static retrieval to interactive dialogue, clinical composition remains misaligned with real-world needs. Although 42% of the corpus referenced objective data, this was polarized toward wellness-focused wearable signals (17.7%); complex diagnostic inputs remained rare, including laboratory values (5.2%), imaging (3.8%), and raw medical records (0.6%). Safety-critical scenarios were effectively absent: suicide/self-harm queries comprised <0.7% of the corpus and chronic disease management only 5.5%. Benchmarks also neglected vulnerable populations (pediatrics/older adults <11%) and global health needs.
  Conclusions: Evaluation benchmarks remain misaligned with real-world clinical needs, lacking raw clinical artifacts, adequate representation of vulnerable populations, and longitudinal chronic care scenarios. The field must adopt standardized query profiling--analogous to clinical trial reporting--to align evaluation with the full complexity of clinical practice.

</details>


### [25] [Consumer-to-Clinical Language Shifts in Ambient AI Draft Notes and Clinician-Finalized Documentation: A Multi-level Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18327)
*Ha Na Cho,Yawen Guo,Sairam Sutari,Emilie Chow,Steven Tam,Danielle Perret,Deepti Pandita,Kai Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究量化了临床医生如何将AI生成的消费者导向用语转换为标准化临床术语，发现约6%的笔记部分存在字典确认的转换，转换强度因临床医生和笔记部分而异，支持开发section感知的ambient AI系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究临床医生如何修改人工智能生成的临床笔记草稿，将面向患者的日常用语转换为标准化临床术语，以改进ambient AI系统的设计。

Method: 使用字典确认的转换框架，量化71,173对AI生成的草稿与最终定稿笔记段落（来自34,726次就诊），确认转换定义为在同一段落中用字典映射的临床等价词替换消费者表达用语。

Result: 编辑显著降低了所有部分的术语密度(p<0.001)；评估和计划部分占最大转换量(59.3%)；共识别7,576次转换事件，分布在4,114个笔记部分(5.8%)，代表1.2%的消费者术语删除；转换强度因临床医生而异(p<0.001)。

Conclusion: 临床医生的后期编辑表现出从对话措辞向标准化、符合部分要求的临床术语的一致性转变，支持section感知的ambient AI设计。

Abstract: Ambient AI generates draft clinical notes from patient-clinician conversations, often using lay or consumer-oriented phrasing to support patient understanding instead of standardized clinical terminology. How clinicians revise these drafts for professional documentation conventions remains unclear. We quantified clinician editing for consumer-to- clinical normalization using a dictionary-confirmed transformation framework. We analyzed 71,173 AI-draft and finalized-note section pairs from 34,726 encounters. Confirmed transformations were defined as replacing a consumer expression with its dictionary-mapped clinical equivalent in the same section. Editing significantly reduced terminology density across all sections (p < 0.001). The Assessment and Plan accounted for the largest transformation volume (59.3%). Our analysis identified 7,576 transformation events across 4,114 note sections (5.8%), representing 1.2% consumer-term deletions. Transformation intensity varied across individual clinicians (p < 0.001). Overall, clinician post-editing demonstrates consistent shifts from conversational phrasing toward standardized, section- appropriate clinical terminology, supporting section-aware ambient AI design.

</details>


### [26] [FaithSteer-BENCH: A Deployment-Aligned Stress-Testing Benchmark for Inference-Time Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18329)
*Zikang Ding,Qiying Hu,Yi Zhang,Hongji Li,Junchi Yao,Hongbo Liu,Lijie Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了FaithSteer-BENCH基准测试，通过三个门控标准（可控性、效用保持和鲁棒性）在固定部署运行点下评估LLM推理时转向方法，揭示了现有方法在标准评估中掩盖的系统性失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究认为简单的激活级干预可以可靠地诱导目标行为变化，但这些结论是在相对宽松的评估设置下得出的，忽视了部署约束、能力权衡和真实世界鲁棒性。

Method: 构建了FaithSteer-BENCH压力测试基准，通过三个门控标准评估转向方法：可控性、效用保持和鲁棒性。测试包括多种模型和代表性转向方法，并进行机制级诊断。

Result: 发现了多个系统性失败模式：虚假可控性、对无关能力的可衡量认知负担、在轻度指令级扰动、角色提示、编码转换和数据稀缺下的显著脆弱性。现有方法在部署导向的实际设置中并未提供可靠的可控性。

Conclusion: 许多转向方法诱导的是提示条件对齐而非稳定的潜在方向偏移，这进一步解释了其脆弱性。FaithSteer-BENCH为未来方法设计、可靠性评估和部署导向的转向研究提供了统一基准和更清晰的分析视角。

Abstract: Inference-time steering is widely regarded as a lightweight and parameter-free mechanism for controlling large language model (LLM) behavior, and prior work has often suggested that simple activation-level interventions can reliably induce targeted behavioral changes. However, such conclusions are typically drawn under relatively relaxed evaluation settings that overlook deployment constraints, capability trade-offs, and real-world robustness. We therefore introduce \textbf{FaithSteer-BENCH}, a stress-testing benchmark that evaluates steering methods at a fixed deployment-style operating point through three gate-wise criteria: controllability, utility preservation, and robustness. Across multiple models and representative steering approaches, we uncover several systematic failure modes that are largely obscured under standard evaluation, including illusory controllability, measurable cognitive tax on unrelated capabilities, and substantial brittleness under mild instruction-level perturbations, role prompts, encoding transformations, and data scarcity. Gate-wise benchmark results show that existing methods do not necessarily provide reliable controllability in deployment-oriented practical settings. In addition, mechanism-level diagnostics indicate that many steering methods induce prompt-conditional alignment rather than stable latent directional shifts, further explaining their fragility under stress. FaithSteer-BENCH therefore provides a unified benchmark and a clearer analytical lens for future method design, reliability evaluation, and deployment-oriented research in steering.

</details>


### [27] [Interpretability without actionability: mechanistic methods cannot correct language model errors despite near-perfect internal representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18353)
*Sanjay Basu,Sadiq Y. Patel,Parth Sheth,Bhairavi Muralidharan,Namrata Elamaran,Aakriti Kinra,John Morgan,Rajaie Batniji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究测试了四种机械可解释性方法（概念瓶颈转向、稀疏自编码器特征转向、对数透镜激活修补、线性探针+真值分离向量转向）能否弥合语言模型中"知识-行动差距"。使用400个临床案例（144个危险、256个良性）进行测试，发现线性探针准确率达98.2% AUROC，但模型输出敏感度仅45.1%，存在53个百分点的知识-行动差距。当前方法无法可靠地将内部知识转化为修正后的输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在内部表示中编码了超出其输出表现的任务相关知识，但机械可解释性方法能否弥合这一"知识-行动差距"尚未得到系统测试。这对于假设可解释性能够实现有效错误修正的AI安全框架具有重要意义。

Method: 比较四种机械可解释性方法：1)概念瓶颈转向(Steerling-8B)；2)稀疏自编码器特征转向；3)对数透镜激活修补；4)线性探针+真值分离向量转向(Qwen 2.5 7B Instruct)。使用400个医师裁判的临床案例（144个危险、256个良性）评估修正假阴性分诊错误的能力。

Result: 线性探针区分危险与良性案例的AUROC达98.2%，但模型输出敏感度仅45.1%，存在53个百分点的知识-行动差距。概念瓶颈转向修正了20%的遗漏危险但扰乱了53%的正确检测，与随机扰动无显著差异(p=0.84)。SAE特征转向尽管发现3695个显著特征但未产生任何效果。高强度TSV转向修正24%遗漏危险同时扰乱6%正确检测，但仍有76%错误未修正。

Conclusion: 当前机械可解释性方法无法可靠地将内部知识转化为修正后的输出，这对假设可解释性能够实现有效错误修正的AI安全框架具有重要影响。研究表明仅凭模型内部存在知识不足以实现可修正性，需要新的方法来激活和利用这些知识。

Abstract: Language models encode task-relevant knowledge in internal representations that far exceeds their output performance, but whether mechanistic interpretability methods can bridge this knowledge-action gap has not been systematically tested. We compared four mechanistic interpretability methods -- concept bottleneck steering (Steerling-8B), sparse autoencoder feature steering, logit lens with activation patching, and linear probing with truthfulness separator vector steering (Qwen 2.5 7B Instruct) -- for correcting false-negative triage errors using 400 physician-adjudicated clinical vignettes (144 hazards, 256 benign). Linear probes discriminated hazardous from benign cases with 98.2% AUROC, yet the model's output sensitivity was only 45.1%, a 53-percentage-point knowledge-action gap. Concept bottleneck steering corrected 20% of missed hazards but disrupted 53% of correct detections, indistinguishable from random perturbation (p=0.84). SAE feature steering produced zero effect despite 3,695 significant features. TSV steering at high strength corrected 24% of missed hazards while disrupting 6% of correct detections, but left 76% of errors uncorrected. Current mechanistic interpretability methods cannot reliably translate internal knowledge into corrected outputs, with implications for AI safety frameworks that assume interpretability enables effective error correction.

</details>


### [28] [LGESynthNet: Controlled Scar Synthesis for Improved Scar Segmentation in Cardiac LGE-MRI Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18356)
*Athira J. Jacob,Puneet Sharma,Daniel Rueckert*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LGESynthNet是一个基于潜在扩散模型的心脏MRI增强合成框架，通过ControlNet架构和奖励模型实现对增强大小、位置和透壁程度的精细控制，仅用429张图像训练即可生成高质量合成样本，用于数据增强可显著提升分割（+6分）和检测（+20分）性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LGE心脏MRI的增强分割对诊断各种缺血性和非缺血性心肌病至关重要，但创建像素级标注极具挑战性且耗时，导致注释数据稀缺。现有生成模型（尤其是扩散模型）依赖大型训练数据集，且难以对细小或局部特征进行精细条件控制。

Method: 提出LGESynthNet，一种基于潜在扩散的可控增强合成框架。该框架采用inpainting方式，使用ControlNet架构，集成了：(a)用于条件特定监督的奖励模型，(b)用于解剖学描述文本提示的标注模块，(c)生物医学文本编码器。训练数据仅需429张图像（79例患者），并使用质量控制滤波器筛选高条件保真度的输出。

Result: 模型能够生成逼真、解剖学上连贯的样本，可显式控制增强的大小、位置和透壁程度。当用于训练数据增强时，筛选后的样本可显著提升下游分割和检测性能，分别提升最高6和20个百分点。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了在小规模数据集上训练高质量医学图像合成模型的可行性，通过条件控制机制和奖励模型实现了对增强特征的精确操控。合成数据作为训练增强可有效改善下游任务的性能，为解决医学图像标注数据稀缺问题提供了新思路。

Abstract: Segmentation of enhancement in LGE cardiac MRI is critical for diagnosing various ischemic and non-ischemic cardiomyopathies. However, creating pixel-level annotations for these images is challenging and labor-intensive, leading to limited availability of annotated data. Generative models, particularly diffusion models, offer promise for synthetic data generation, yet many rely on large training datasets and often struggle with fine-grained conditioning control, especially for small or localized features. We introduce LGESynthNet, a latent diffusion-based framework for controllable enhancement synthesis, enabling explicit control over size, location, and transmural extent. Formulated as inpainting using a ControlNet-based architecture, the model integrates: (a) a reward model for conditioning-specific supervision, (b) a captioning module for anatomically descriptive text prompts, and (c) a biomedical text encoder. Trained on just 429 images (79 patients), it produces realistic, anatomically coherent samples. A quality control filter selects outputs with high conditioning-fidelity, which when used for training augmentation, improve downstream segmentation and detection performance, by up-to 6 and 20 points respectively.

</details>


### [29] [From Weak Cues to Real Identities: Evaluating Inference-Driven De-Anonymization in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18382)
*Myeongseob Ko,Jihyun Jeong,Sumiran Singh Thakur,Gyuhak Kim,Ruoxi Jia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究揭示了LLM智能体带来的新型隐私威胁——推理驱动链接(inference-driven linkage)。智能体可以通过组合分散的、非识别性的线索与公开信息来重构真实世界身份，绕过传统匿名化保护。该研究在Netflix Prize、AOL数据集和InferLink基准测试中验证了这种威胁，智能体在Netflix设置下成功重构79.2%的身份，显著优于56.0%的传统基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上认为匿名化是实用的隐私保护手段，因为重新识别匿名记录需要领域专业知识、定制算法和人工佐证，成本很高。然而，LLM智能体的出现可能削弱这一屏障。研究旨在系统评估LLM智能体是否能够自主从分散的、单独来看不具有识别性的线索中重构真实世界身份。

Method: 将这种威胁形式化为"推理驱动链接"(inference-driven linkage)，并在三个设置中进行系统评估：1) 经典链接场景(Netflix和AOL)；2) InferLink(一个受控基准测试，可变参数包括任务意图、共享线索和攻击者知识)；3. 现代富文本人工制品。评估不依赖任务特定启发式方法。

Result: 智能体成功执行了固定池匹配和开放域身份解析任务。在Netflix Prize设置中，智能体重构了79.2%的身份，显著优于56.0%的传统基线。链接不仅在明确对抗性提示下出现，也作为InferLink和非结构化研究叙事中跨源分析的副产物出现。

Conclusion: 研究表明，身份推理——不仅是明确的信息披露——必须被视为一级隐私风险；隐私评估必须衡量智能体能推断出什么身份。

Abstract: Anonymization is widely treated as a practical safeguard because re-identifying anonymous records was historically costly, requiring domain expertise, tailored algorithms, and manual corroboration. We study a growing privacy risk that may weaken this barrier: LLM-based agents can autonomously reconstruct real-world identities from scattered, individually non-identifying cues. By combining these sparse cues with public information, agents resolve identities without bespoke engineering. We formalize this threat as \emph{inference-driven linkage} and systematically evaluate it across three settings: classical linkage scenarios (Netflix and AOL), \emph{InferLink} (a controlled benchmark varying task intent, shared cues, and attacker knowledge), and modern text-rich artifacts. Without task-specific heuristics, agents successfully execute both fixed-pool matching and open-ended identity resolution. In the Netflix Prize setting, an agent reconstructs 79.2\% of identities, significantly outperforming a 56.0\% classical baseline. Furthermore, linkage emerges not only under explicit adversarial prompts but also as a byproduct of benign cross-source analysis in \emph{InferLink} and unstructured research narratives. These findings establish that identity inference -- not merely explicit information disclosure -- must be treated as a first-class privacy risk; evaluations must measure what identities an agent can infer.

</details>


### [30] [From Topic to Transition Structure: Unsupervised Concept Discovery at Corpus Scale via Predictive Associative Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18420)
*Jason Dury*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于时间共现的关联空间模型，通过对比学习从文本过渡结构中提取"功能概念"（what text does），与基于主题的嵌入聚类形成互补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有嵌入模型按语义内容（what text is about）对文本分组，作者试图发现文本的过渡结构概念（what text does），即文本的运作方式、功能和修辞模式。

Method: 在9,766本Project Gutenberg书籍（2496万段落）上训练29.4M参数的对比模型，学习373M共现对，将预训练嵌入映射到关联空间。在容量约束下（42.75%准确率），模型必须跨递归模式压缩而非记忆单个共现。在k=50至k=2000六个粒度进行聚类。

Result: 构建多分辨率概念图，从"直接对抗"和"抒情沉思"等宏观模式到"水手方言"和"法庭交叉询问"等精确语域和场景模板。k=100时每个聚类平均包含4,508本书（总计9,766本），确认了语料库级别的模式。与嵌入相似度聚类对比表明：原始嵌入按主题分组，关联空间按功能、语域和文学传统分组。

Conclusion: 无需重新训练即可将未见小说分配到现有聚类；关联模型将每本小说集中到连贯的聚类子集，而原始嵌入分配则饱和几乎所有聚类。该方法将预测关联记忆(PAM)从情景回忆扩展到概念形成，在不同机制下产生定性不同的行为。

Abstract: Embedding models group text by semantic content, what text is about. We show that temporal co-occurrence within texts discovers a different kind of structure: recurrent transition-structure concepts or what text does. We train a 29.4M-parameter contrastive model on 373 million co-occurrence pairs from 9,766 Project Gutenberg texts (24.96 million passages), mapping pre-trained embeddings into an association space where passages with similar transition structure cluster together. Under capacity constraint (42.75% accuracy), the model must compress across recurring patterns rather than memorise individual co-occurrences. Clustering at six granularities (k=50 to k=2,000) produces a multi-resolution concept map; from broad modes like "direct confrontation" and "lyrical meditation" to precise registers and scene templates like "sailor dialect" and "courtroom cross-examination." At k=100, clusters average 4,508 books each (of 9,766), confirming corpus-wide patterns. Direct comparison with embedding-similarity clustering shows that raw embeddings group by topic while association-space clusters group by function, register, and literary tradition. Unseen novels are assigned to existing clusters without retraining; the association model concentrates each novel into a selective subset of coherent clusters, while raw embedding assignment saturates nearly all clusters. Validation controls address positional, length, and book-concentration confounds. The method extends Predictive Associative Memory (PAM, arXiv:2602.11322) from episodic recall to concept formation: where PAM recalls specific associations, multi-epoch contrastive training under compression extracts structural patterns that transfer to unseen texts, the same framework producing qualitatively different behaviour in a different regime.

</details>


### [31] [Prune-then-Quantize or Quantize-then-Prune? Understanding the Impact of Compression Order in Joint Model Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18426)
*Minjun Kim,Jaehyeon Choi,Hyunwoo Yang,Jongjin Kim,Jinho Song,U Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了联合模型压缩中压缩顺序的影响问题，提出了"渐进强度假设"，即较弱扰动应先于较强扰动应用，并从理论和实验两方面验证了该假设的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联合模型压缩已成为提高模型效率的有效策略，但压缩顺序（不同压缩方法在管道中的序列）这一核心因素尚未被充分探索。之前的研究要么假设技术之间的正交性而回避了这个问题，要么只在高度约束的情况下进行研究。因此，压缩顺序在塑造模型性能方面的更广泛作用仍然没有被很好地理解。

Method: 1. 将优化压缩顺序问题形式化；2. 提出渐进强度假设（Progressive Intensity Hypothesis），认为较弱扰动应先于较强扰动应用；3. 提供理论保证，表明一种顺序相对于另一种顺序的收益随着底层性能差距的增加而增加；4. 在语言和视觉模型上进行广泛实验验证。

Result: 实验验证了假设的有效性，并进一步表明该假设在更广泛的设置中具有普遍性，如多阶段压缩和混合精度量化。

Conclusion: 渐进强度假设在联合模型压缩中具有普遍适用性，为优化压缩顺序提供了理论指导和实践依据。

Abstract: What happens when multiple compression methods are combined-does the order in which they are applied matter? Joint model compression has emerged as a powerful strategy to achieve higher efficiency by combining multiple methods such as pruning and quantization. A central but underexplored factor in joint model compression is the compression order, or the sequence of different methods within the compression pipeline. Most prior studies have either sidestepped the issue by assuming orthogonality between techniques, while a few have examined them only in highly constrained cases. Consequently, the broader role of compression order in shaping model performance remains poorly understood. In this paper, we address the overlooked problem of compression order and provide both theoretical and empirical analysis. We formulate the problem of optimizing the compression order and introduce the Progressive Intensity Hypothesis, which states that weaker perturbations should precede stronger ones. We provide theoretical guarantees showing that the relative benefit of one order increases with the underlying performance gap. Extensive experiments on both language and vision models validate the hypothesis, and further show its generality to broader setups such as multi-stage compression and mixed-precision quantization.

</details>


### [32] [AS2 -- Attention-Based Soft Answer Sets: An End-to-End Differentiable Neuro-Soft-Symbolic Reasoning Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18436)
*Wael AbdAlmageed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AS2是一种完全可微的神经符号架构，将离散的ASP求解器替换为软连续的T_P算子近似，通过最小化概率提升T_P的固定点残差进行端到端训练，在视觉数独和MNIST加法任务上取得99.89%和99.7%以上的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经符号AI系统通常将神经感知模块与离散符号求解器通过不可微边界耦合，约束满足反馈无法在训练时到达感知编码器，限制了端到端可微性和系统性能。

Method: 提出AS2架构，用软连续的ASP即时结果算子T_P近似替代离散求解器；维持有限符号域上每位置概率分布；通过最小化概率提升T_P的固定点残差实现端到端训练；使用约束组 membership embeddings编码问题结构，摒弃传统位置嵌入。

Result: 视觉数独任务：99.89%单元格准确率，100%约束满足率（Clingo验证）；MNIST加法（N∈{2,4,8}）：所有规模下数字准确率均>99.7%。

Conclusion: 软可微固定点算子结合约束感知注意力和声明式约束规范可匹配或超越基于流水线和求解器的神经符号系统，同时保持完全端到端可微性。

Abstract: Neuro-symbolic artificial intelligence (AI) systems typically couple a neural perception module to a discrete symbolic solver through a non-differentiable boundary, preventing constraint-satisfaction feedback from reaching the perception encoder during training. We introduce AS2 (Attention-Based Soft Answer Sets), a fully differentiable neuro-symbolic architecture that replaces the discrete solver with a soft, continuous approximation of the Answer Set Programming (ASP) immediate consequence operator $T_P$. AS2 maintains per-position probability distributions over a finite symbol domain throughout the forward pass and trains end-to-end by minimizing the fixed-point residual of a probabilistic lift of $T_P$, thereby differentiating through the constraint check without invoking an external solver at either training or inference time. The architecture is entirely free of conventional positional embeddings. Instead, it encodes problem structure through constraint-group membership embeddings that directly reflect the declarative ASP specification, making the model agnostic to arbitrary position indexing. On Visual Sudoku, AS2 achieves 99.89% cell accuracy and 100% constraint satisfaction (verified by Clingo) across 1,000 test boards, using a greedy constrained decoding procedure that requires no external solver. On MNIST Addition with $N \in \{2, 4, 8\}$ addends, AS2 achieves digit accuracy above 99.7% across all scales. These results demonstrate that a soft differentiable fixpoint operator, combined with constraint-aware attention and declarative constraint specification, can match or exceed pipeline and solver-based neuro-symbolic systems while maintaining full end-to-end differentiability.

</details>


### [33] [AlignMamba-2: Enhancing Multimodal Fusion and Sentiment Analysis with Modality-Aware Mamba](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18462)
*Yan Li,Yifei Xing,Xiangyuan Lan,Xin Li,Haifeng Chen,Dongmei Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对大模型时代下情感计算任务中的多模态融合效率与异构性问题，提出AlignMamba-2框架。该方法结合最优传输距离与最大均值差异的双重对齐策略，并设计模态感知Mamba层（采用专家混合架构），在CMU-MOSI、CMU-MOSEI、NYU-Depth V2和MVSA-Single四个数据集上实现了动态时序分析与静态图文分类任务的最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大模型时代，将通用知识有效迁移到特定情感计算任务面临挑战，尤其涉及计算效率与多模态异构性。Transformer方法虽能建模模态间依赖，但二次计算复杂度限制长序列处理；Mamba模型虽高效，但难以捕捉跨模态对齐所需的全域非序列关系。

Method: 提出AlignMamba-2框架，包含：1) 双重对齐策略（最优传输距离+最大均值差异）促进模态间几何与统计一致性，无推理时间开销；2) 模态感知Mamba层采用专家混合架构，包含模态特定专家与模态共享专家，显式处理融合过程中的数据异构性。

Result: 在四个具有挑战性的基准数据集上验证：动态时序数据集（CMU-MOSI、CMU-MOSEI）与静态图像相关任务（NYU-Depth V2、MVSA-Single），在从动态时序分析到静态图文分类的多样化模式识别任务中建立了新的最先进水平。

Conclusion: AlignMamba-2通过双重对齐策略与模态感知Mamba层有效解决了Mamba模型在跨模态全局关系捕捉上的局限，同时保持计算效率，在多模态融合与情感分析任务中实现了效果与效率的统一。

Abstract: In the era of large-scale pre-trained models, effectively adapting general knowledge to specific affective computing tasks remains a challenge, particularly regarding computational efficiency and multimodal heterogeneity. While Transformer-based methods have excelled at modeling inter-modal dependencies, their quadratic computational complexity limits their use with long-sequence data. Mamba-based models have emerged as a computationally efficient alternative; however, their inherent sequential scanning mechanism struggles to capture the global, non-sequential relationships that are crucial for effective cross-modal alignment. To address these limitations, we propose \textbf{AlignMamba-2}, an effective and efficient framework for multimodal fusion and sentiment analysis. Our approach introduces a dual alignment strategy that regularizes the model using both Optimal Transport distance and Maximum Mean Discrepancy, promoting geometric and statistical consistency between modalities without incurring any inference-time overhead. More importantly, we design a Modality-Aware Mamba layer, which employs a Mixture-of-Experts architecture with modality-specific and modality-shared experts to explicitly handle data heterogeneity during the fusion process. Extensive experiments on four challenging benchmarks, including dynamic time-series (on the CMU-MOSI and CMU-MOSEI datasets) and static image-related tasks (on the NYU-Depth V2 and MVSA-Single datasets), demonstrate that AlignMamba-2 establishes a new state-of-the-art in both effectiveness and efficiency across diverse pattern recognition tasks, ranging from dynamic time-series analysis to static image-text classification.

</details>


### [34] [Cognitive Mismatch in Multimodal Large Language Models for Discrete Symbol Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18472)
*Yinghui Li,Jiayi Kuang,Peng Xing,Daixian Liu,Junnan Dong,Shu-Yu Guo,Yangning Li,Qingyu Zhou,Wenhao Jiang,Hai-Tao Zheng,Ying Shen,Liang Lin,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个综合基准测试，用于评估顶级多模态大语言模型(MLLMs)在离散符号语义空间中的理解能力，涵盖语言、文化、数学、物理和化学五个领域。研究发现模型存在"认知错位"现象：常在基础符号识别上失败，却在复杂推理任务中成功，表明其依赖语言概率而非真正的视觉感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在自然场景理解方面取得显著成功，但其处理离散符号的能力仍是关键开放问题。离散符号（如数学公式、化学结构、语言字符）需要精确、更深层的解释，而不同于连续视觉数据。

Method: 构建了综合基准测试，评估顶级MLLMs在五个领域（语言、文化、数学、物理、化学）中的"离散语义空间"导航能力。

Result: 发现了一个反直觉现象：模型经常在基础符号识别上失败，却在复杂推理任务中成功，这表明它们依赖语言概率而非真正的视觉感知。

Conclusion: 揭示了当前AI能力的重要差距：难以真正感知和理解支撑科学发现和抽象思维的符号语言。为开发更严谨、更符合人类智能的系统提供了路线图。

Abstract: While Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have achieved remarkable success in interpreting natural scenes, their ability to process discrete symbols -- the fundamental building blocks of human cognition -- remains a critical open question. Unlike continuous visual data, symbols such as mathematical formulas, chemical structures, and linguistic characters require precise, deeper interpretation. This paper introduces a comprehensive benchmark to evaluate how top-tier MLLMs navigate these "discrete semantic spaces" across five domains: language, culture, mathematics, physics, and chemistry. Our investigation uncovers a counterintuitive phenomenon: models often fail at basic symbol recognition yet succeed in complex reasoning tasks, suggesting they rely on linguistic probability rather than true visual perception. By exposing this "cognitive mismatch", we highlight a significant gap in current AI capabilities: the struggle to truly perceive and understand the symbolic languages that underpin scientific discovery and abstract thought. This work offers a roadmap for developing more rigorous, human-aligned intelligent systems.

</details>


### [35] [Cross-Domain Demo-to-Code via Neurosymbolic Counterfactual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18495)
*Jooyoung Kim,Wonje Choi,Younguk Song,Honguk Woo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NeSyCR是一个神经符号反事实推理框架,用于实现视频指导的机器人编程的跨域适配。该方法通过将视频演示抽象为符号轨迹,并利用反事实状态识别跨域不兼容性,从而在部署环境中实现可靠的任务程序适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉语言模型(VLM)缺乏程序理解能力,无法在跨域场景下重新 formulation 因果依赖关系并实现任务兼容的行为。视频指导的机器人编程面临演示环境与部署环境之间的感知和物理差异导致的程序不匹配问题。

Method: NeSyCR框架包含三个核心步骤:1)将视频演示抽象为捕获底层任务程序的符号轨迹;2)给定部署观察,推导反事实状态以揭示跨域不兼容性;3)通过可验证检查在符号状态空间中探索,提出恢复演示程序兼容性的程序修订。

Result: NeSyCR在任务成功率上比最强基线Statler提升了31.14%,在模拟和真实世界的操作任务中展现出稳健的跨域适配能力。

Conclusion: NeSyCR通过神经符号方法实现了可信的代码策略合成,为视频指导机器人编程中的跨域适配问题提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have enabled video-instructed robotic programming, allowing agents to interpret video demonstrations and generate executable control code. We formulate video-instructed robotic programming as a cross-domain adaptation problem, where perceptual and physical differences between demonstration and deployment induce procedural mismatches. However, current VLMs lack the procedural understanding needed to reformulate causal dependencies and achieve task-compatible behavior under such domain shifts. We introduce NeSyCR, a neurosymbolic counterfactual reasoning framework that enables verifiable adaptation of task procedures, providing a reliable synthesis of code policies. NeSyCR abstracts video demonstrations into symbolic trajectories that capture the underlying task procedure. Given deployment observations, it derives counterfactual states that reveal cross-domain incompatibilities. By exploring the symbolic state space with verifiable checks, NeSyCR proposes procedural revisions that restore compatibility with the demonstrated procedure. NeSyCR achieves a 31.14% improvement in task success over the strongest baseline Statler, showing robust cross-domain adaptation across both simulated and real-world manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [36] [Correlation-Weighted Multi-Reward Optimization for Compositional Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18528)
*Jungmyung Wi,Hyunsoo Kim,Donghyun Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为相关性加权多奖励优化（CWMRO）的方法，用于解决文本到图像模型在组合生成中的挑战。该方法通过利用概念奖励之间的相关性结构，自适应地加权每个属性概念，从而平衡竞争性奖励信号，提高多概念提示的组合生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到图像模型在组合生成方面面临长期挑战。模型往往难以满足单个提示中的多个概念，经常遗漏某些概念导致部分成功。这种失败凸显了在奖励优化过程中联合优化多个概念的困难性，因为相互竞争的概念会相互干扰。

Method: 1. 将多概念提示分解为预定义的概念组（如对象、属性和关系）；2. 从专门的奖励模型获取每个概念的奖励信号；3. 使用基于相关性的难度估计，自适应地重新加权这些奖励，为冲突或难以满足的概念分配更高权重；4. 聚焦于每个组内最具挑战性的概念，鼓励模型同时满足所有请求的属性。

Result: 在具有挑战性的多概念基准测试上展示了一致的改进，包括ConceptMix、GenEval 2和T2I-CompBench。

Conclusion: 通过考虑概念之间的相互作用，CWMRO能够平衡竞争性奖励信号，并强调部分满足但在样本中不一致生成的概念，从而提高了组合生成效果。该方法在SD3.5和FLUX.1-dev上进行了验证。

Abstract: Text-to-image models produce images that align well with natural language prompts, but compositional generation has long been a central challenge. Models often struggle to satisfy multiple concepts within a single prompt, frequently omitting some concepts and resulting in partial success. Such failures highlight the difficulty of jointly optimizing multiple concepts during reward optimization, where competing concepts can interfere with one another.
